    <section class="bg-blue-dark audience-preview">
  <div class="container">
    <div class="row">
      <div class="col-xs-12 text-center">
        
        <h2>The Microsoft Search Network audience</h2>
        
<p>
          The Microsoft Search Network is a part of your customers' lives, powering the devices, apps and sites they use every day and meeting them in the moments that matter most. Find out more about the Microsoft Search Network audience in the U.S. and how users are searching from their desktop computers <span class="nowrap">below.<button aria-label="Citation 1 for Microsoft Search Network audience data" class="footnote" type="button" data-original-title="" data-toggle="popover" data-content="Comscore qSearch, Explicit Core Search (custom), March 2020, United States, desktop traffic only.
" data-placement="top" data-container="body" aria-expanded="false" data-isopen="false">1</button>
</span>
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>

          <h3 class="show-as-h4"><span class="countTo" data-speed="3000">129</span> million</h3>
          <p>unique desktop searchers on the Microsoft Search Network<sup>1</sup></p>
